TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）在分布外组合偏移下失败，不是因为摊销推理问题，而是因为字典学习失败，导致字典方向错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性表示假设下，稀疏自编码器在分布外组合偏移中的失败原因。传统观点认为SAE的失败源于摊销推理（将稀疏推理固定为编码器）带来的系统差距，但本文旨在验证这一假设并探索更深层原因。

Method: 通过控制实验分解失败原因：1）比较SAE与传统稀疏编码方法（使用逐样本迭代推理如FISTA）；2）在不同训练集大小、潜在维度和稀疏度水平下测试；3）将SAE编码器替换为在相同字典上的逐样本FISTA推理；4）使用oracle基线验证问题可解性。

Result: 1）SAE的摊销差距在所有测试规模下持续存在；2）SAE在分布外组合偏移下失败；3）关键约束是字典学习而非推理过程：SAE学习的字典方向严重错误；4）在相同字典上使用FISTA替代编码器无法缩小差距；5）oracle基线证明只要有良好字典，问题在所有测试规模下都可解。

Conclusion: SAE失败应重新定义为字典学习挑战，而非摊销问题。可扩展的字典学习是叠加下稀疏推理的关键开放问题。研究重点应从改进摊销推理转向开发更有效的字典学习方法。

Abstract: The linear representation hypothesis states that neural network activations encode high-level concepts as linear mixtures. However, under superposition, this encoding is a projection from a higher-dimensional concept space into a lower-dimensional activation space, and a linear decision boundary in the concept space need not remain linear after projection. In this setting, classical sparse coding methods with per-sample iterative inference leverage compressed sensing guarantees to recover latent factors. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs), on the other hand, amortise sparse inference into a fixed encoder, introducing a systematic gap. We show this amortisation gap persists across training set sizes, latent dimensions, and sparsity levels, causing SAEs to fail under out-of-distribution (OOD) compositional shifts. Through controlled experiments that decompose the failure, we identify dictionary learning -- not the inference procedure -- as the binding constraint: SAE-learned dictionaries point in substantially wrong directions, and replacing the encoder with per-sample FISTA on the same dictionary does not close the gap. An oracle baseline proves the problem is solvable with a good dictionary at all scales tested. Our results reframe the SAE failure as a dictionary learning challenge, not an amortisation problem, and point to scalable dictionary learning as the key open problem for sparse inference under superposition.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Koopman Operator Identification of Model Parameter Trajectories for Temporal Domain Generalization (KOMET)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26923)
*Randy C. Hoover,Jacob James,Paul May,Kyle Caudle*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: KOMET框架利用Koopman算子识别模型参数在时间域漂移下的演化规律，通过EDMD方法从参数轨迹中学习线性动力学系统，实现无需重新训练的自适应预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳环境中部署的参数模型会因数据分布随时间演化（时间域漂移）而性能下降，需要一种无需未来标注数据就能自适应预测模型参数演化的方法。

Method: 将训练参数序列视为非线性动力系统轨迹，通过EDMD识别其线性Koopman算子；采用热启动顺序训练确保参数轨迹平滑性，使用傅里叶增强的可观测量字典捕捉分布漂移的周期性结构。

Result: 在六个数据集（旋转、振荡和扩展分布几何）上评估，KOMET在100个保留时间步上实现0.981至1.000的平均自主展开精度；谱分析和耦合分析揭示了与漂移决策边界几何一致的动态结构。

Conclusion: KOMET提供了一种模型无关、数据驱动的方法，通过识别参数演化的Koopman算子实现零重新训练的自适应，为时间域漂移问题提供了可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Parametric models deployed in non-stationary environments degrade as the underlying data distribution evolves over time (a phenomenon known as temporal domain drift). In the current work, we present KOMET (Koopman Operator identification of Model parameter Evolution under Temporal drift), a model-agnostic, data-driven framework that treats the sequence of trained parameter vectors as the trajectory of a nonlinear dynamical system and identifies its governing linear operator via Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD). A warm-start sequential training protocol enforces parameter-trajectory smoothness, and a Fourier-augmented observable dictionary exploits the periodic structure inherent in many real-world distribution drifts. Once identified, KOMET's Koopman operator predicts future parameter trajectories autonomously, without access to future labeled data, enabling zero-retraining adaptation at deployment. Evaluated on six datasets spanning rotating, oscillating, and expanding distribution geometries, KOMET achieves mean autonomous-rollout accuracies between 0.981 and 1.000 over 100 held-out time steps. Spectral and coupling analyses further reveal interpretable dynamical structure consistent with the geometry of the drifting decision boundary.

</details>


### [114] [Static and Dynamic Approaches to Computing Barycenters of Probability Measures on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26940)
*David Gentile,James M. Murphy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼几何的图支撑概率测度重心编码模型，通过动态最优传输的黎曼结构在单纯形上实现内在梯度下降，用于图支撑测度的合成与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输几何在图支撑测度上会退化，需要一种能够在图结构上有效定义测度加权平均（重心）的方法，为机器学习和计算机视觉中的信号处理提供工具。

Method: 利用动态最优传输问题诱导的黎曼结构在单纯形上实现内在梯度下降：1）通过计算作用最小化曲线近似黎曼指数映射及其逆；2）通过近似当前迭代与参考测度间的测地线计算方差泛函梯度；3）通过连续性方程离散化向前推送迭代；4）通过计算目标与参考测度间测地线构建二次规划进行分析。

Result: 与基于静态最优传输熵正则化（使用图距离函数编码图结构）的方法相比，本文方法在数值实验中表现良好，验证了内在梯度下降在概率单纯形上为图支撑测度合成与分析提供了连贯框架。

Conclusion: 在概率单纯形上的内在梯度下降为图支撑测度的合成与分析提供了一个连贯的框架，通过动态最优传输的黎曼几何克服了经典最优传输在图结构上的退化问题。

Abstract: The optimal transportation problem defines a geometry of probability measures which leads to a definition for weighted averages (barycenters) of measures, finding application in the machine learning and computer vision communities as a signal processing tool. Here, we implement a barycentric coding model for measures which are supported on a graph, a context in which the classical optimal transport geometry becomes degenerate, by leveraging a Riemannian structure on the simplex induced by a dynamic formulation of the optimal transport problem. We approximate the exponential mapping associated to the Riemannian structure, as well as its inverse, by utilizing past approaches which compute action minimizing curves in order to numerically approximate transport distances for measures supported on discrete spaces. Intrinsic gradient descent is then used to synthesize barycenters, wherein gradients of a variance functional are computed by approximating geodesic curves between the current iterate and the reference measures; iterates are then pushed forward via a discretization of the continuity equation. Analysis of measures with respect to given dictionary of references is performed by solving a quadratic program formed by computing geodesics between target and reference measures. We compare our novel approach to one based on entropic regularization of the static formulation of the optimal transport problem where the graph structure is encoded via graph distance functions, we present numerical experiments validating our approach, and we conclude that intrinsic gradient descent on the probability simplex provides a coherent framework for the synthesis and analysis of measures supported on graphs.

</details>


### [115] [Online Statistical Inference of Constant Sample-averaged Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26982)
*Saunak Kumar Panda,Tong Li,Ruiqi Liu,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个基于样本平均Q学习的统计在线推断框架，使用随机缩放构建置信区间，并在网格世界和动态资源匹配问题上验证性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习算法在决策任务中广泛应用，但其性能容易受到高方差和不稳定性的影响，特别是在噪声环境或稀疏奖励场景下。需要开发统计推断方法来量化Q值估计的不确定性。

Method: 提出样本平均Q学习的统计在线推断框架，应用泛函中心极限定理(FCLT)在一般条件下推导改进算法的渐近分布，通过随机缩放技术构建Q值的置信区间。

Result: 在网格世界（简单玩具问题）和动态资源匹配（实际应用问题）两个问题上进行实验，比较改进方法和传统Q学习方法的覆盖率和置信区间宽度。

Conclusion: 提出的统计在线推断框架能够为样本平均Q学习提供有效的置信区间估计，有助于量化强化学习算法的不确定性，在噪声和稀疏奖励环境中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning algorithms have been widely used for decision-making tasks in various domains. However, the performance of these algorithms can be impacted by high variance and instability, particularly in environments with noise or sparse rewards. In this paper, we propose a framework to perform statistical online inference for a sample-averaged Q-learning approach. We adapt the functional central limit theorem (FCLT) for the modified algorithm under some general conditions and then construct confidence intervals for the Q-values via random scaling. We conduct experiments to perform inference on both the modified approach and its traditional counterpart, Q-learning using random scaling and report their coverage rates and confidence interval widths on two problems: a grid world problem as a simple toy example and a dynamic resource-matching problem as a real-world example for comparison between the two solution approaches.

</details>


### [116] [Parameter Estimation in Stochastic Differential Equations via Wiener Chaos Expansion and Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27019)
*Francisco Delgado-Vences,José Julián Pavón-Español,Arelly Ornelas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于Wiener混沌展开和随机梯度下降的SDE参数估计方法，将随机推断问题转化为确定性优化问题，显著提高了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模拟的SDE参数估计方法（如MCMC或MLE）计算负担重、采样要求高，需要一种更高效的计算框架来处理随机微分方程的参数反演问题。

Method: 采用Wiener混沌展开将随机解投影到Hermite多项式的正交基上，将随机动力学转化为确定性函数（传播子）的层次系统，然后通过正则化差异函数最小化，使用随机梯度下降进行参数估计。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在各种非线性SDE（包括个体生物生长模型）上都能准确恢复参数，即使从离散、噪声观测数据中也能获得良好结果，证明了其鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: WCE-SGD框架为复杂随机系统的高效建模提供了重要范式转变，通过将随机推断转化为确定性优化问题，显著降低了计算负担和采样要求。

Abstract: This study addresses the inverse problem of parameter estimation for Stochastic Differential Equations (SDEs) by minimizing a regularized discrepancy functional via Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD). To achieve computational efficiency, we leverage the Wiener Chaos Expansion (WCE), a spectral decomposition technique that projects the stochastic solution onto an orthogonal basis of Hermite polynomials. This transformation effectively maps the stochastic dynamics into a hierarchical system of deterministic functions, termed the \textit{propagator}. By reducing the stochastic inference task to a deterministic optimization problem, our framework circumvents the heavy computational burden and sampling requirements of traditional simulation-based methods like MCMC or MLE. The robustness and scalability of the proposed approach are demonstrated through numerical experiments on various non-linear SDEs, including models for individual biological growth. Results show that the WCE-SGD framework provides accurate parameter recovery even from discrete, noisy observations, offering a significant paradigm shift in the efficient modeling of complex stochastic systems.

</details>


### [117] [Overcoming the Incentive Collapse Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27049)
*Qichuan Yin,Ziwei Su,Shuangning Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出哨兵审计支付机制，在预算约束下维持可控的人类努力水平，并开发激励感知的主动统计推断框架，优化审计率和任务分配以最小化统计损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助任务委派中，人类努力成本高且不可观测，基于准确性的支付方案存在激励崩溃问题：随着AI准确性提高，维持人类积极努力需要无限支付。

Method: 提出哨兵审计支付机制，确保有限成本下维持可控的人类努力水平；在此基础上开发激励感知的主动统计推断框架，联合优化审计率、主动采样和跨难度任务的预算分配。

Result: 实验表明，相比标准主动学习和仅审计基线，该方法在成本-误差权衡方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 哨兵审计机制解决了激励崩溃问题，激励感知的主动统计推断框架在预算约束下实现了更好的成本-误差权衡，为AI辅助任务委派系统提供了有效的激励设计。

Abstract: AI-assisted task delegation is increasingly common, yet human effort in such systems is costly and typically unobserved. Recent work by Bastani and Cachon (2025); Sambasivan et al. (2021) shows that accuracy-based payment schemes suffer from incentive collapse: as AI accuracy improves, sustaining positive human effort requires unbounded payments. We study this problem in a budget-constrained principal-agent framework with strategic human agents whose output accuracy depends on unobserved effort. We propose a sentinel-auditing payment mechanism that enforces a strictly positive and controllable level of human effort at finite cost, independent of AI accuracy. Building on this incentive-robust foundation, we develop an incentive-aware active statistical inference framework that jointly optimizes (i) the auditing rate and (ii) active sampling and budget allocation across tasks of varying difficulty to minimize the final statistical loss under a single budget. Experiments demonstrate improved cost-error tradeoffs relative to standard active learning and auditing-only baselines.

</details>


### [118] [Bayes-MICE: A Bayesian Approach to Multiple Imputation for Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27142)
*Amuche Ibenegbu,Pierre Lafaye de Micheaux,Rohitash Chandra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯MICE方法，通过MCMC采样处理时间序列缺失数据，结合时间滞后特征，相比基线方法降低插补误差并量化不确定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分析常受缺失数据影响，传统MICE方法缺乏对参数不确定性的考虑，且未充分利用时间序列的时序特性

Method: 扩展MICE为贝叶斯框架(Bayes-MICE)，使用MCMC采样（RWM和MALA两种采样器）处理参数和插补值的不确定性，加入时间感知初始化和时间滞后特征以尊重时序特性

Result: 在AirQuality和PhysioNet数据集上评估，Bayes-MICE相比基线方法在所有变量上均降低插补误差，MALA比RWM收敛更快且提供更一致的后验探索，同时能有效量化插补过程的不确定性

Conclusion: Bayes-MICE框架为时间序列插补提供了实用高效的方法，在提高准确性的同时能有效量化不确定性，适用于环境和临床等多种场景

Abstract: Time-series analysis is often affected by missing data, a common problem across several fields, including healthcare and environmental monitoring.
  Multiple Imputation by Chained Equations (MICE) has been prominent for imputing missing values through "fully conditional specification". We extend MICE using the Bayesian framework (Bayes-MICE), utilising Bayesian inference to impute missing values via Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling to account for uncertainty in MICE model parameters and imputed values. We also include temporally informed initialisation and time-lagged features in the model to respect the sequential nature of time-series data. We evaluate the Bayes-MICE method using two real-world datasets (AirQuality and PhysioNet), and using both the Random Walk Metropolis (RWM) and the Metropolis-Adjusted Langevin Algorithm (MALA) samplers. Our results demonstrate that Bayes-MICE reduces imputation errors relative to the baseline methods over all variables and accounts for uncertainty in the imputation process, thereby providing a more accurate measure of imputation error. We also found that MALA converges faster than RWM, achieving comparable accuracy while providing more consistent posterior exploration. Overall, these findings suggest that the Bayes-MICE framework represents a practical and efficient approach to time-series imputation, balancing increased accuracy with meaningful quantification of uncertainty in various environmental and clinical settings.

</details>


### [119] [Statistical Guarantees for Distributionally Robust Optimization with Optimal Transport and OT-Regularized Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27871)
*Jeremiah Birrell,Xiaoxi Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于最优传输（OT）和OT正则化散度模型邻域的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）的有限样本统计性能保证，推导了监督学习中DRO对抗训练的集中不等式，适用于广泛的OT成本函数，包括软约束范数球OT成本函数和OT正则化f-散度模型邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注p-Wasserstein距离的DRO性能保证，但缺乏对更广泛的OT成本函数（特别是软约束范数球OT成本函数）的理论分析。此外，OT正则化f-散度模型邻域结合了对抗样本生成和对抗重加权机制，这种组合在实证中显示出更好的性能，但缺乏理论保证。

Method: 作者推导了监督学习中DRO对抗训练的集中不等式，这些结果适用于广泛的OT成本函数，包括：1）软约束范数球OT成本函数，2）OT正则化f-散度模型邻域（结合对抗样本生成和对抗重加权）。即使在p-Wasserstein情况下，他们的边界在DRO邻域大小方面也表现出比先前结果更好的行为。

Result: 1）首次覆盖软约束范数球OT成本函数的理论保证；2）首次应用于OT正则化f-散度模型邻域（结合对抗样本生成和对抗重加权）；3）即使在p-Wasserstein情况下，边界在DRO邻域大小方面也优于先前结果；4）结果适用于广泛的OT成本函数，超越了先前仅限于p-Wasserstein距离的研究。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于最优传输的分布鲁棒优化提供了更全面的有限样本统计性能保证，特别针对软约束OT成本函数和OT正则化散度模型邻域，填补了理论空白，并为对抗训练中观察到的经验优势提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study finite-sample statistical performance guarantees for distributionally robust optimization (DRO) with optimal transport (OT) and OT-regularized divergence model neighborhoods. Specifically, we derive concentration inequalities for supervised learning via DRO-based adversarial training, as commonly employed to enhance the adversarial robustness of machine learning models. Our results apply to a wide range of OT cost functions, beyond the $p$-Wasserstein case studied by previous authors. In particular, our results are the first to: 1) cover soft-constraint norm-ball OT cost functions; soft-constraint costs have been shown empirically to enhance robustness when used in adversarial training, 2) apply to the combination of adversarial sample generation and adversarial reweighting that is induced by using OT-regularized $f$-divergence model neighborhoods; the added reweighting mechanism has also been shown empirically to further improve performance. In addition, even in the $p$-Wasserstein case, our bounds exhibit better behavior as a function of the DRO neighborhood size than previous results when applied to the adversarial setting.

</details>


### [120] [Persistence diagrams of random matrices via Morse theory: universality and a new spectral diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27903)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对称矩阵M的二次型在单位球面上的子水平集滤过的持久图完全由M的特征值决定，并将随机矩阵理论的普适性转移到持久图普适性，建立了持久熵作为新的谱诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将拓扑数据分析中的持久同调应用于随机矩阵理论，探索特征值统计的拓扑表征，并开发比传统谱统计量更有效的诊断工具。

Method: 通过Morse理论分析二次型f(x)=x^T M x在单位球面S^{n-1}上的子水平集滤过，证明持久图由矩阵特征值完全决定。将随机矩阵理论（GOE、GUE、Wishart）的普适性转移到持久图，推导持久熵的闭式表达式，并通过数值模拟验证统计特性。

Result: 持久图有n-1个有限条带，第k个条带位于同调维度k-1，长度等于第k个特征值间距s_k=λ_{k+1}-λ_k。对于GOE矩阵，持久熵PE=log(8n/π)-1，持久统计量的变异系数以n^{-0.6}衰减。持久熵在区分GOE和GUE矩阵方面优于标准水平间距比⟨r⟩（AUC 0.978 vs 0.952，n=100），并能检测Rosenzweig-Porter模型中的全局谱扰动。

Conclusion: 持久熵作为新的谱诊断工具，捕捉了与现有随机矩阵理论工具互补的信息，为随机矩阵普适性类提供了拓扑指纹，在谱分析和矩阵分类中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We prove that the persistence diagram of the sublevel set filtration of the quadratic form f(x) = x^T M x restricted to the unit sphere S^{n-1} is analytically determined by the eigenvalues of the symmetric matrix M. By Morse theory, the diagram has exactly n-1 finite bars, with the k-th bar living in homological dimension k-1 and having length equal to the k-th eigenvalue spacing s_k = λ_{k+1} - λ_k. This identification transfers random matrix theory (RMT) universality to persistence diagram universality: for matrices drawn from the Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE), we derive the closed-form persistence entropy PE = log(8n/π) - 1, and verify numerically that the coefficient of variation of persistence statistics decays as n^{-0.6}. Different random matrix ensembles (GOE, GUE, Wishart) produce distinct universal persistence diagrams, providing topological fingerprints of RMT universality classes. As a practical consequence, we show that persistence entropy outperforms the standard level spacing ratio \langle r \rangle for discriminating GOE from GUE matrices (AUC 0.978 vs. 0.952 at n = 100, non-overlapping bootstrap 95% CIs), and detects global spectral perturbations in the Rosenzweig-Porter model to which \langle r \rangle is blind. These results establish persistence entropy as a new spectral diagnostic that captures complementary information to existing RMT tools.

</details>


### [121] [LDDMM stochastic interpolants: an application to domain uncertainty quantification in hemodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28324)
*Sarah Katz,Francesco Romor,Jia-Jie Zhu,Alfonso Caiazzo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件随机插值的三维形状生成建模框架，利用LDDMM配准学习几何间的条件漂移，通过拉回和推前算子扩展到复杂形状和不同域上的随机变量，应用于心血管模拟中从患者队列生成主动脉形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三维生物医学形状的数据增强问题，以及为给定形状生成可控幅度的随机扰动，这对于量化医学图像分割引起的域不确定性对生物标志物估计的影响至关重要。

Method: 基于条件随机插值框架，利用LDDMM配准方法学习几何间的条件漂移，通过拉回和推前算子将方法扩展到复杂形状和不同域上的随机变量。条件变量由中心线点和相应内切球半径组成的潜在几何表示定义。

Result: 开发了一种能够从初始患者队列生成主动脉形状的方法，实现了三维生物医学形状的数据增强，并能生成可控幅度的形状随机扰动。

Conclusion: 该方法为三维生物医学形状的生成建模提供了有效框架，特别适用于心血管模拟中的不确定性量化，能够评估医学图像分割对生物标志物估计的影响。

Abstract: We introduce a novel conditional stochastic interpolant framework for generative modeling of three-dimensional shapes. The method builds on a recent LDDMM-based registration approach to learn the conditional drift between geometries. By leveraging the resulting pull-back and push-forward operators, we extend this formulation beyond standard Cartesian grids to complex shapes and random variables defined on distinct domains. We present an application in the context of cardiovascular simulations, where aortic shapes are generated from an initial cohort of patients. The conditioning variable is a latent geometric representation defined by a set of centerline points and the radii of the corresponding inscribed spheres. This methodology facilitates both data augmentation for three-dimensional biomedical shapes, and the generation of random perturbations of controlled magnitude for a given shape. These capabilities are essential for quantifying the impact of domain uncertainties arising from medical image segmentation on the estimation of relevant biomarkers.

</details>


### [122] [Functional Natural Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28681)
*Aurelien Bibaut,Houssam Zenati,Thibaud Rahier,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种交叉拟合去偏方法用于离线策略学习，实现√N遗憾界，即使对于复杂度超过Donsker类的策略类，前提是误差乘积项为O(N^{-1/2})


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线策略学习中高复杂度策略类的学习问题，传统方法在策略类复杂度超过Donsker类时难以获得√N收敛率

Method: 提出交叉拟合去偏装置，通过误差分解将遗憾界分解为策略误差因子和环境动态复杂度因子，两者可相互权衡

Result: 获得√N遗憾界，即使策略类复杂度超过Donsker类，前提是误差乘积项满足O(N^{-1/2})收敛率

Conclusion: 交叉拟合去偏方法为离线策略学习提供了理论保证，明确展示了策略类复杂度和环境动态复杂度之间的权衡关系

Abstract: We propose a cross-fitted debiasing device for policy learning from offline data. A key consequence of the resulting learning principle is $\sqrt N$ regret even for policy classes with complexity greater than Donsker, provided a product-of-errors nuisance remainder is $O(N^{-1/2})$. The regret bound factors into a plug-in policy error factor governed by policy-class complexity and an environment nuisance factor governed by the complexity of the environment dynamics, making explicit how one may be traded against the other.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Multiverse: Language-Conditioned Multi-Game Level Blending via Shared Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26782)
*In-Chang Baek,Jiyun Jung,Sung-Hyun Kim,Geum-Hwan Hwang,Kyung-Joong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Multiverse是一个语言条件多游戏关卡生成器，通过文本指令实现跨游戏关卡融合，使用共享潜在空间对齐文本和关卡结构，支持可控的跨游戏内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到关卡生成器通常局限于单一游戏领域，需要扩展语言条件生成到多个游戏，学习能够捕捉跨领域结构关系的表示。

Method: 提出Multiverse模型，学习共享潜在空间对齐文本指令和关卡结构，使用基于阈值的多正例对比监督连接跨游戏的语义相关关卡，通过潜在插值和组合文本提示实现可控融合。

Result: 学习到的表示支持可控的跨游戏关卡融合，显著提高了同类型游戏内的融合质量，为语言条件多游戏内容生成提供了统一表示。

Conclusion: Multiverse通过共享潜在空间和对比监督实现了语言引导的跨游戏关卡融合，为多游戏文本到关卡生成提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Text-to-level generation aims to translate natural language descriptions into structured game levels, enabling intuitive control over procedural content generation. While prior text-to-level generators are typically limited to a single game domain, extending language-conditioned generation to multiple games requires learning representations that capture structural relationships across domains. We propose Multiverse, a language-conditioned multi-game level generator that enables cross-game level blending through textual specifications. The model learns a shared latent space aligning textual instructions and level structures, while a threshold-based multi-positive contrastive supervision links semantically related levels across games. This representation allows language to guide which structural characteristics should be preserved when combining content from different games, enabling controllable blending through latent interpolation and zero-shot generation from compositional textual prompts. Experiments show that the learned representation supports controllable cross-game level blending and significantly improves blending quality within the same game genre, while providing a unified representation for language-conditioned multi-game content generation.

</details>


### [124] [Concerning Uncertainty -- A Systematic Survey of Uncertainty-Aware XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26838)
*Helena Löfström,Tuwe Löfström,Anders Hjort,Fatima Rabia Yapicioglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综述了不确定性感知的可解释人工智能（UAXAI），探讨了不确定性如何融入解释流程、相关方法及其评估实践，提出了统一评估原则的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前UAXAI领域缺乏系统性分析，不确定性量化方法多样但评估实践分散，需要建立连接不确定性传播、鲁棒性和人类决策的统一评估框架。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统分析UAXAI领域的三类主要不确定性量化方法（贝叶斯、蒙特卡洛、保形方法）和三种不确定性整合策略（可信度评估、模型/解释约束、不确定性显式传达）。

Result: 发现评估实践碎片化且以模型为中心，对用户关注有限，可靠性属性报告不一致；近期研究趋向校准、分布无关技术，并将解释器变异性作为核心关注点。

Conclusion: UAXAI的进展需要统一评估原则，连接不确定性传播、鲁棒性和人类决策，反事实和校准方法是实现可解释性与可靠性对齐的有前景途径。

Abstract: This paper surveys uncertainty-aware explainable artificial intelligence (UAXAI), examining how uncertainty is incorporated into explanatory pipelines and how such methods are evaluated. Across the literature, three recurring approaches to uncertainty quantification emerge (Bayesian, Monte Carlo, and Conformal methods), alongside distinct strategies for integrating uncertainty into explanations: assessing trustworthiness, constraining models or explanations, and explicitly communicating uncertainty. Evaluation practices remain fragmented and largely model centered, with limited attention to users and inconsistent reporting of reliability properties (e.g., calibration, coverage, explanation stability). Recent work leans towards calibration, distribution free techniques and recognizes explainer variability as a central concern. We argue that progress in UAXAI requires unified evaluation principles that link uncertainty propagation, robustness, and human decision-making, and highlight counterfactual and calibration approaches as promising avenues for aligning interpretability with reliability.

</details>


### [125] [Transparency as Architecture: Structural Compliance Gaps in EU AI Act Article 50 II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26983)
*Vera Schmitt,Niklas Kruse,Premtim Sahitaj,Julius Schöning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 欧盟《人工智能法案》第50条II款要求AI生成内容进行双重透明度标记，但当前生成式AI系统存在结构性障碍无法合规，需要将透明度作为架构设计需求而非事后标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析欧盟《人工智能法案》第50条II款关于AI生成内容双重透明度要求（人类可理解和机器可读标记）与当前生成式AI系统实际约束之间的冲突，识别合规障碍并提出解决方案。

Method: 使用合成数据生成和自动事实核查作为诊断用例，分析双重透明度要求在实际应用中的可行性问题，识别结构性障碍。

Result: 发现三个结构性障碍：(a) 缺乏跨平台标记格式支持人机交错输出；(b) 法规的"可靠性"标准与概率性模型行为不匹配；(c) 缺少针对不同用户专业水平的披露适应指南。合规不能简化为事后标记。

Conclusion: 解决合规问题需要将透明度视为架构设计需求，而非事后标记，需要跨学科研究（法律语义、AI工程、人本设计）来弥合这些结构性差距。

Abstract: Art. 50 II of the EU Artificial Intelligence Act mandates dual transparency for AI-generated content: outputs must be labeled in both human-understandable and machine-readable form for automated verification. This requirement, entering into force in August 2026, collides with fundamental constraints of current generative AI systems. Using synthetic data generation and automated fact-checking as diagnostic use cases, we show that compliance cannot be reduced to post-hoc labeling. In fact-checking pipelines, provenance tracking is not feasible under iterative editorial workflows and non-deterministic LLM outputs; moreover, the assistive-function exemption does not apply, as such systems actively assign truth values rather than supporting editorial presentation. In synthetic data generation, persistent dual-mode marking is paradoxical: watermarks surviving human inspection risk being learned as spurious features during training, while marks suited for machine verification are fragile under standard data processing. Across both domains, three structural gaps obstruct compliance: (a) absent cross-platform marking formats for interleaved human-AI outputs; (b) misalignment between the regulation's 'reliability' criterion and probabilistic model behavior; and (c) missing guidance for adapting disclosures to heterogeneous user expertise. Closing these gaps requires transparency to be treated as an architectural design requirement, demanding interdisciplinary research across legal semantics, AI engineering, and human-centered desi

</details>


### [126] [When Verification Hurts: Asymmetric Effects of Multi-Agent Feedback in Logic Proof Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27076)
*Tahreem Yasir,Sutapa Dey Tithi,Benyamin Tabarsi,Dmitri Droujkov,Sam Gilson Yasitha Rajapaksha,Xiaoyi Tian,Arun Ramesh,DongKuan,Xu,Tiffany Barnes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了LLMs在命题逻辑证明辅导中的可靠性，发现验证机制仅在反馈准确率低时有效，而在反馈已可靠时反而会降低性能，并识别了所有模型在复杂度4-5以上的共享能力上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于自动化辅导，但它们在结构化符号领域（如命题逻辑证明）的可靠性尚不清楚。现有辅导评估通常依赖模型自评估或二元正确性判断，缺乏对反馈质量的细粒度分析。

Method: 引入基于知识图谱的基准测试，包含516个独特证明状态及其步骤级标注和难度指标。评估三种角色专业化管道：Tutor（部分解决方案访问）、Teacher（完整推导访问）和Judge（验证Tutor反馈）。通过对比已验证解决方案路径来分析反馈质量。

Result: 发现显著的不对称性：当上游反馈容易出错（准确率<70%）时，验证能改善结果；但当反馈已可靠（准确率>85%）时，验证反而通过过度规范化使性能下降4-6个百分点。所有模型和管道在复杂度4-5以上的证明状态上都存在共享的能力上限。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了"添加验证器或更丰富上下文能普遍改善辅导"的假设，提出了需要自适应、难度感知的架构，根据估计的复杂度和上游可靠性来路由问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated tutoring, but their reliability in structured symbolic domains remains unclear. We study step-level feedback for propositional logic proofs, which require precise symbolic reasoning aligned with a learner's current proof state. We introduce a knowledge-graph-grounded benchmark of 516 unique proof states with step-level annotations and difficulty metrics. Unlike prior tutoring evaluations that rely on model self-assessment or binary correctness, our framework enables fine-grained analysis of feedback quality against verified solution paths. We evaluate three role-specialized pipelines with varying solution access: Tutor (partial solution access), Teacher (full derivation access), and Judge (verification of Tutor feedback). Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: verification improves outcomes when upstream feedback is error-prone (<70% accuracy), but degrades performance by 4-6 percentage points through over-specification when feedback is already reliable (>85%). Critically, we identify a shared complexity ceiling; no model or pipeline reliably succeeds on proof states exceeding complexity 4-5. These findings challenge the assumption that adding verifiers or richer context universally improves tutoring, motivating adaptive, difficulty-aware architectures that route problems by estimated complexity and upstream reliability.

</details>


### [127] [The Price of Meaning: Why Every Semantic Memory System Forgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27116)
*Sambartha Ray Barman,Andrey Starenky,Sofia Bodnar,Nikhil Narasimhan,Ashwin Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文证明语义记忆系统存在不可避免的干扰-泛化权衡：语义组织促进泛化但必然导致干扰、遗忘和错误回忆


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI记忆系统按语义组织信息以实现泛化、类比和概念检索，但这种组织方式存在代价。论文旨在形式化分析语义记忆系统中不可避免的干扰-遗忘-错误回忆权衡

Method: 提出"语义连续核阈值记忆"的形式化框架，推导四个理论结果：1)语义有用表示具有有限有效秩；2)有限局部维度导致检索邻域存在正竞争者质量；3)记忆增长时保持率衰减为零；4)δ-凸关联诱饵条件下错误回忆无法通过阈值调整消除。在五种架构上验证预测

Result: 理论推导得到验证：纯语义系统直接表现为遗忘和错误回忆；推理增强系统部分缓解症状但将优雅退化转化为灾难性失败；完全避免干扰的系统牺牲了语义泛化能力。所有测试架构都无法避免干扰代价

Conclusion: 语义记忆存在根本性的干扰-泛化权衡：语义组织促进泛化的几何结构必然导致干扰、遗忘和错误回忆。这是语义记忆不可避免的代价，任何架构都无法完全避免

Abstract: Every major AI memory system in production today organises information by meaning. That organisation enables generalisation, analogy, and conceptual retrieval -- but it comes at a price. We prove that the same geometric structure enabling semantic generalisation makes interference, forgetting, and false recall inescapable. We formalise this tradeoff for \textit{semantically continuous kernel-threshold memories}: systems whose retrieval score is a monotone function of an inner product in a semantic feature space with finite local intrinsic dimension.
  Within this class we derive four results: (1) semantically useful representations have finite effective rank; (2) finite local dimension implies positive competitor mass in retrieval neighbourhoods; (3) under growing memory, retention decays to zero, yielding power-law forgetting curves under power-law arrival statistics; (4) for associative lures satisfying a $δ$-convexity condition, false recall cannot be eliminated by threshold tuning.
  We test these predictions across five architectures: vector retrieval, graph memory, attention-based context, BM25 filesystem retrieval, and parametric memory. Pure semantic systems express the vulnerability directly as forgetting and false recall. Reasoning-augmented systems partially override these symptoms but convert graceful degradation into catastrophic failure. Systems that escape interference entirely do so by sacrificing semantic generalisation.
  The price of meaning is interference, and no architecture we tested avoids paying it.

</details>


### [128] [MediHive: A Decentralized Agent Collective for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27150)
*Xiaoyang Wang,Christopher C. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MediHive：一种用于医疗问答的去中心化多智能体框架，通过共享内存池和迭代融合机制，在MedQA和PubMedQA数据集上优于单智能体和集中式基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体LLM系统在处理复杂、跨学科的医疗推理任务时存在不足，而现有的集中式多智能体系统存在可扩展性瓶颈、单点故障和角色混淆等问题。去中心化多智能体系统在医疗领域应用不足，需要开发能够处理不确定性和冲突证据的鲁棒医疗推理框架

Method: 提出MediHive框架：1）部署基于LLM的智能体，自主分配专业角色；2）进行初步分析；3）通过基于条件的证据辩论检测分歧；4）通过多轮本地融合同伴见解达成共识；5）集成共享内存池与迭代融合机制

Result: 在MedQA和PubMedQA数据集上分别达到84.3%和78.4%的准确率，优于单LLM和集中式基线方法

Conclusion: MediHive推进了医疗AI领域可扩展、容错的去中心化多智能体系统，解决了集中式设计的关键限制，在推理密集型任务中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized medical reasoning tasks, yet single-agent systems often falter on complex, interdisciplinary problems requiring robust handling of uncertainty and conflicting evidence. Multi-agent systems (MAS) leveraging LLMs enable collaborative intelligence, but prevailing centralized architectures suffer from scalability bottlenecks, single points of failure, and role confusion in resource-constrained environments. Decentralized MAS (D-MAS) promise enhanced autonomy and resilience via peer-to-peer interactions, but their application to high-stakes healthcare domains remains underexplored. We introduce MediHive, a novel decentralized multi-agent framework for medical question answering that integrates a shared memory pool with iterative fusion mechanisms. MediHive deploys LLM-based agents that autonomously self-assign specialized roles, conduct initial analyses, detect divergences through conditional evidence-based debates, and locally fuse peer insights over multiple rounds to achieve consensus. Empirically, MediHive outperforms single-LLM and centralized baselines on MedQA and PubMedQA datasets, attaining accuracies of 84.3% and 78.4%, respectively. Our work advances scalable, fault-tolerant D-MAS for medical AI, addressing key limitations of centralized designs while demonstrating superior performance in reasoning-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [129] [daVinci-LLM:Towards the Science of Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27164)
*Yiwei Qin,Yixiu Liu,Tiantian Mi,Muhang Xie,Zhen Huang,Weiye Si,Pengrui Lu,Siyuan Feng,Xia Wu,Liming Liu,Ye Luo,Jinlong Hou,Qipeng Guo,Yu Qiao,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: daVinci-LLM项目填补了大规模预训练研究的空白，通过工业级资源与学术自由的结合，采用完全开放的科学方法论，系统探索了数据处理的深度、领域饱和动态、组合平衡等关键维度对预训练能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练阶段决定了模型的能力上限，但这一领域存在结构性悖论：拥有计算资源的商业组织因商业压力缺乏透明度，而学术机构虽有研究自由但缺乏预训练规模的计算资源。daVinci-LLM旨在填补这一空白，结合工业级资源与完全研究自由，推进预训练科学。

Method: 采用完全开放的范式作为科学方法论，发布完整的数据处理流水线、训练过程和系统探索结果。使用Data Darwinism框架（L0-L9分类法）进行数据筛选到合成的原则性处理。训练3B参数模型从随机初始化开始，使用8T tokens的两阶段自适应课程学习，从基础能力逐步转向推理密集型增强。通过200+控制消融实验进行系统分析。

Result: 通过系统实验发现：数据处理深度系统性增强能力，成为与规模扩展同等重要的关键维度；不同领域展现独特的饱和动态，需要从比例调整到格式转换的自适应策略；组合平衡支持针对性强化同时防止性能崩溃；评估协议选择显著影响对预训练进展的理解。

Conclusion: 通过发布完整的探索过程，使社区能够基于研究发现和系统方法论构建累积性科学知识。daVinci-LLM项目展示了结合工业资源与学术自由的价值，为预训练科学提供了系统化的研究框架和实证基础。

Abstract: The foundational pretraining phase determines a model's capability ceiling, as post-training struggles to overcome capability foundations established during pretraining, yet it remains critically under-explored. This stems from a structural paradox: organizations with computational resources operate under commercial pressures that inhibit transparent disclosure, while academic institutions possess research freedom but lack pretraining-scale computational resources. daVinci-LLM occupies this unexplored intersection, combining industrial-scale resources with full research freedom to advance the science of pretraining. We adopt a fully-open paradigm that treats openness as scientific methodology, releasing complete data processing pipelines, full training processes, and systematic exploration results. Recognizing that the field lacks systematic methodology for data processing, we employ the Data Darwinism framework, a principled L0-L9 taxonomy from filtering to synthesis. We train a 3B-parameter model from random initialization across 8T tokens using a two-stage adaptive curriculum that progressively shifts from foundational capabilities to reasoning-intensive enhancement. Through 200+ controlled ablations, we establish that: processing depth systematically enhances capabilities, establishing it as a critical dimension alongside volume scaling; different domains exhibit distinct saturation dynamics, necessitating adaptive strategies from proportion adjustments to format shifts; compositional balance enables targeted intensification while preventing performance collapse; how evaluation protocol choices shape our understanding of pretraining progress. By releasing the complete exploration process, we enable the community to build upon our findings and systematic methodologies to form accumulative scientific knowledge in pretraining.

</details>


### [130] [AutoMS: Multi-Agent Evolutionary Search for Cross-Physics Inverse Microstructure Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27195)
*Zhenyuan Zhao,Yu Xing,Tianyang Xue,Lingxin Cao,Xin Yan,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoMS：多智能体神经符号框架，将跨物理微结构逆设计重构为LLM驱动的进化搜索，通过模拟感知进化搜索解决传统方法的物理幻觉问题，在17个任务上达到83.8%成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨物理微结构逆设计面临巨大、不连续的搜索空间，传统拓扑优化计算成本过高，而深度生成模型存在"物理幻觉"问题，无法保证严格的物理有效性。需要一种既能理解语义设计意图又能确保物理有效性的方法。

Method: 提出AutoMS多智能体神经符号框架，将LLM作为"语义导航器"初始化搜索空间并打破局部最优，同时引入模拟感知进化搜索（SAES）利用模拟反馈进行局部梯度近似和定向参数更新。系统包含Manager、Parser、Generator和Simulator四个专门智能体。

Result: 在17个多样化跨物理任务上达到83.8%的成功率，几乎是传统NSGA-II（43.7%）的两倍，显著优于基于ReAct的LLM基线（53.3%）。分层架构将总执行时间减少了23.3%。

Conclusion: AutoMS证明自主智能体系统能够有效导航复杂的物理景观，弥合语义设计意图与严格物理有效性之间的差距，为跨物理微结构逆设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Designing microstructures that satisfy coupled cross-physics objectives is a fundamental challenge in material science. This inverse design problem involves a vast, discontinuous search space where traditional topology optimization is computationally prohibitive, and deep generative models often suffer from "physical hallucinations," lacking the capability to ensure rigorous validity. To address this limitation, we introduce AutoMS, a multi-agent neuro-symbolic framework that reformulates inverse design as an LLM-driven evolutionary search. Unlike methods that treat LLMs merely as interfaces, AutoMS integrates them as "semantic navigators" to initialize search spaces and break local optima, while our novel Simulation-Aware Evolutionary Search (SAES) addresses the "blindness" of traditional evolutionary strategies. Specifically, SAES utilizes simulation feedback to perform local gradient approximation and directed parameter updates, effectively guiding the search toward physically valid Pareto frontiers. Orchestrating specialized agents (Manager, Parser, Generator, and Simulator), AutoMS achieves a state-of-the-art 83.8\% success rate on 17 diverse cross-physics tasks, nearly doubling the performance of traditional NSGA-II (43.7\%) and significantly outperforming ReAct-based LLM baselines (53.3\%). Furthermore, our hierarchical architecture reduces total execution time by 23.3\%. AutoMS demonstrates that autonomous agent systems can effectively navigate complex physical landscapes, bridging the gap between semantic design intent and rigorous physical validity.

</details>


### [131] [Quantification of Credal Uncertainty: A Distance-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27270)
*Xabier Gonzalez-Garcia,Siu Lun Chau,Julian Rodemann,Michele Caprio,Krikamol Muandet,Humberto Bustince,Sébastien Destercke,Eyke Hüllermeier,Yusuf Sale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于距离的方法来量化信度集的总不确定性、偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，特别针对多类分类问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信度集（概率测度的闭凸集）能自然表示机器学习中的偶然和认知不确定性，但如何量化这两种不确定性，特别是在多类分类中，仍缺乏探索

Method: 提出基于距离的方法，在积分概率度量（IPM）框架内引入一系列不确定性度量。具体使用总变差距离实例化该框架，获得简单高效的多类分类不确定性度量

Result: 所得度量具有清晰的语义解释，满足理论要求，计算上易于处理。在二分类情况下能恢复已有的不确定性度量，并提供了其多类推广。实验证实了实用性和低计算成本下的良好性能

Conclusion: 提出的距离基方法为信度集的不确定性量化提供了理论合理且计算高效的多类分类解决方案，填补了现有不确定性度量在多类推广方面的空白

Abstract: Credal sets, i.e., closed convex sets of probability measures, provide a natural framework to represent aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty in machine learning. Yet how to quantify these two types of uncertainty for a given credal set, particularly in multiclass classification, remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose a distance-based approach to quantify total, aleatoric, and epistemic uncertainty for credal sets. Concretely, we introduce a family of such measures within the framework of Integral Probability Metrics (IPMs). The resulting quantities admit clear semantic interpretations, satisfy natural theoretical desiderata, and remain computationally tractable for common choices of IPMs. We instantiate the framework with the total variation distance and obtain simple, efficient uncertainty measures for multiclass classification. In the binary case, this choice recovers established uncertainty measures, for which a principled multiclass generalization has so far been missing. Empirical results confirm practical usefulness, with favorable performance at low computational cost.

</details>


### [132] [Self-evolving AI agents for protein discovery and directed evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27303)
*Yang Tan,Lingrong Zhang,Mingchen Li,Yuanxi Yu,Bozitao Zhong,Bingxin Zhou,Nanqing Dong,Liang Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VenusFactory2是一个自主蛋白质科学发现框架，通过自演化的多智能体基础设施实现动态工作流合成，超越现有智能体并在VenusAgentEval基准测试中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质科学发现受限于人工协调信息和算法，而通用智能体在复杂领域项目中能力不足，需要更专业的自主框架来应对蛋白质相关需求

Method: 采用自演化的多智能体基础设施，从静态工具使用转向动态工作流合成，能够根据单个自然语言提示自主组织和优化蛋白质发现过程

Result: 在VenusAgentEval基准测试中超越了一系列知名智能体，能够自主组织蛋白质的发现和优化过程

Conclusion: VenusFactory2提供了一个有效的自主蛋白质科学发现框架，通过动态工作流合成解决了传统方法的局限性，在蛋白质相关任务中表现出色

Abstract: Protein scientific discovery is bottlenecked by the manual orchestration of information and algorithms, while general agents are insufficient in complex domain projects. VenusFactory2 provides an autonomous framework that shifts from static tool usage to dynamic workflow synthesis via a self-evolving multi-agent infrastructure to address protein-related demands. It outperforms a set of well-known agents on the VenusAgentEval benchmark, and autonomously organizes the discovery and optimization of proteins from a single natural language prompt.

</details>


### [133] [TokenDance: Token-to-Token Music-to-Dance Generation with Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27314)
*Ziyue Yang,Kaixing Yang,Xulong Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TokenDance是一个两阶段的音乐到舞蹈生成框架，通过双模态标记化和高效的标记级生成解决现有数据集覆盖有限导致的泛化能力差问题，在生成质量和推理速度上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D舞蹈数据集覆盖范围有限，导致当前模型只能处理有限的音乐风格和编舞模式，对真实世界音乐的泛化能力差，生成的舞蹈过于简单重复，表现力和真实感严重下降。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段使用有限标量量化离散化舞蹈和音乐，将舞蹈动作分解为上、下半身组件并施加运动学-动力学约束，将音乐分解为语义和声学特征并使用专用码本捕捉编舞特定结构；第二阶段基于双向Mamba骨干构建局部-全局-局部标记到标记生成器，实现连贯动作合成、强音乐-舞蹈对齐和高效非自回归推理。

Result: 广泛实验表明TokenDance在生成质量和推理速度方面均达到整体最先进的性能，突显了其在真实世界音乐到舞蹈应用中的有效性和实用价值。

Conclusion: TokenDance通过双模态标记化和高效的标记级生成，成功解决了现有音乐到舞蹈生成模型的泛化能力限制，为虚拟现实、舞蹈教育和数字角色动画等应用提供了高质量的舞蹈生成解决方案。

Abstract: Music-to-dance generation has broad applications in virtual reality, dance education, and digital character animation. However, the limited coverage of existing 3D dance datasets confines current models to a narrow subset of music styles and choreographic patterns, resulting in poor generalization to real-world music. Consequently, generated dances often become overly simplistic and repetitive, substantially degrading expressiveness and realism.
  To tackle this problem, we present TokenDance, a two-stage music-to-dance generation framework that explicitly addresses this limitation through dual-modality tokenization and efficient token-level generation. In the first stage, we discretize both dance and music using Finite Scalar Quantization, where dance motions are factorized into upper and lower-body components with kinematic-dynamic constraints, and music is decomposed into semantic and acoustic features with dedicated codebooks to capture choreography-specific structures. In the second stage, we introduce a Local-Global-Local token-to-token generator built on a Bidirectional Mamba backbone, enabling coherent motion synthesis, strong music-dance alignment, and efficient non-autoregressive inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TokenDance achieves overall state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in both generation quality and inference speed, highlighting its effectiveness and practical value for real-world music-to-dance applications.

</details>


### [134] [CounterMoral: Editing Morals in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27338)
*Michael Ripa,Jim Davies*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CounterMoral是一个用于评估语言模型道德判断编辑能力的基准数据集，测试不同伦理框架下的模型编辑技术效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然语言模型在事实信息编辑方面取得进展，但道德判断修改这一与人类价值观对齐的关键方面研究不足，需要专门评估工具

Method: 创建CounterMoral基准数据集，应用多种编辑技术到多个语言模型，评估其在修改道德判断方面的表现

Result: 研究结果为评估设计为符合伦理的语言模型提供了贡献

Conclusion: 需要专门工具评估语言模型道德判断编辑能力，CounterMoral基准为此提供了评估框架

Abstract: Recent advancements in language model technology have significantly enhanced the ability to edit factual information. Yet, the modification of moral judgments, a crucial aspect of aligning models with human values, has garnered less attention. In this work, we introduce CounterMoral, a benchmark dataset crafted to assess how well current model editing techniques modify moral judgments across diverse ethical frameworks. We apply various editing techniques to multiple language models and evaluate their performance. Our findings contribute to the evaluation of language models designed to be ethical.

</details>


### [135] [A Comparative Study in Surgical AI: Datasets, Foundation Models, and Barriers to Med-AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27341)
*Kirill Skobelev,Eric Fithian,Yegor Baranovski,Jack Cook,Sandeep Angara,Shauna Otto,Zhuang-Fang Yi,John Zhu,Daniel A. Donoho,X. Y. Han,Neeraj Mainkar,Margaux Masson-Forsythe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前AI模型在手术图像分析任务上表现不佳，即使使用数十亿参数模型和大量训练数据，在神经外科工具检测等看似简单的任务上仍存在显著差距，且扩展模型规模带来的性能提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI在多个生物医学任务上已匹配或超越人类专家，但在手术图像分析方面仍落后。手术需要整合多模态数据、人机交互和物理效应等复杂任务，因此具备通用能力的AI模型作为协作工具具有吸引力，但需要提升性能。

Method: 通过案例研究，使用2026年最先进的AI方法进行手术工具检测，特别是神经外科工具检测。采用数十亿参数模型进行扩展实验，分析模型规模和训练时间对性能指标的影响。

Result: 即使使用数十亿参数模型和大量训练，当前视觉语言模型在神经外科工具检测任务上表现不佳。扩展实验表明，增加模型规模和训练时间仅带来性能指标的边际改善，呈现收益递减趋势。

Conclusion: 当前AI模型在手术应用场景中仍面临显著障碍，这些障碍无法单纯通过增加计算资源来解决，且在不同模型架构中持续存在。这表明数据和标签可用性可能不是唯一限制因素，需要探索新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent Artificial Intelligence (AI) models have matched or exceeded human experts in several benchmarks of biomedical task performance, but have lagged behind on surgical image-analysis benchmarks. Since surgery requires integrating disparate tasks -- including multimodal data integration, human interaction, and physical effects -- generally-capable AI models could be particularly attractive as a collaborative tool if performance could be improved. On the one hand, the canonical approach of scaling architecture size and training data is attractive, especially since there are millions of hours of surgical video data generated per year. On the other hand, preparing surgical data for AI training requires significantly higher levels of professional expertise, and training on that data requires expensive computational resources. These trade-offs paint an uncertain picture of whether and to-what-extent modern AI could aid surgical practice. In this paper, we explore this question through a case study of surgical tool detection using state-of-the-art AI methods available in 2026. We demonstrate that even with multi-billion parameter models and extensive training, current Vision Language Models fall short in the seemingly simple task of tool detection in neurosurgery. Additionally, we show scaling experiments indicating that increasing model size and training time only leads to diminishing improvements in relevant performance metrics. Thus, our experiments suggest that current models could still face significant obstacles in surgical use cases. Moreover, some obstacles cannot be simply ``scaled away'' with additional compute and persist across diverse model architectures, raising the question of whether data and label availability are the only limiting factors. We discuss the main contributors to these constraints and advance potential solutions.

</details>


### [136] [LLM Readiness Harness: Evaluation, Observability, and CI Gates for LLM/RAG Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27355)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于LLM和RAG应用的准备度评估框架，将评估转化为部署决策工作流，通过自动化基准测试、可观测性和质量门控生成场景加权的准备度分数，支持实际部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM和RAG系统的评估通常停留在离线指标报告，缺乏将评估结果转化为实际部署决策的操作性框架。需要一种能够综合考虑质量、性能、成本等多维度因素，并支持风险控制的系统化评估方法。

Method: 开发了一个准备度评估框架，结合自动化基准测试、OpenTelemetry可观测性和CI质量门控，通过最小API契约集成。系统聚合工作流成功率、策略合规性、事实准确性、检索命中率、成本和p95延迟等指标，生成场景加权的准备度分数和帕累托前沿。

Result: 在票务路由工作流和BEIR基准任务（SciFact和FiQA）上进行了全面评估（162/162有效单元格）。结果显示：FiQA任务中，在k=5时gpt-4.1-mini在准备度和忠实度上领先，而gpt-5.2有显著的延迟代价；SciFact任务中模型质量相近但操作上仍可区分。票务路由回归门控能一致拒绝不安全的提示变体，证明框架能阻止风险发布而非仅报告离线分数。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可重复、操作性强的决策框架，用于判断LLM或RAG系统是否准备好部署，将评估从单纯的指标报告提升为实际的部署决策支持工具。

Abstract: We present a readiness harness for LLM and RAG applications that turns evaluation into a deployment decision workflow. The system combines automated benchmarks, OpenTelemetry observability, and CI quality gates under a minimal API contract, then aggregates workflow success, policy compliance, groundedness, retrieval hit rate, cost, and p95 latency into scenario-weighted readiness scores with Pareto frontiers. We evaluate the harness on ticket-routing workflows and BEIR grounding tasks (SciFact and FiQA) with full Azure matrix coverage (162/162 valid cells across datasets, scenarios, retrieval depths, seeds, and models). Results show that readiness is not a single metric: on FiQA under sla-first at k=5, gpt-4.1-mini leads in readiness and faithfulness, while gpt-5.2 pays a substantial latency cost; on SciFact, models are closer in quality but still separable operationally. Ticket-routing regression gates consistently reject unsafe prompt variants, demonstrating that the harness can block risky releases instead of merely reporting offline scores. The result is a reproducible, operationally grounded framework for deciding whether an LLM or RAG system is ready to ship.

</details>


### [137] [Defend: Automated Rebuttals for Peer Review with Minimal Author Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27360)
*Jyotsana Khatri,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEFEND：一种基于LLM的自动反驳生成工具，通过结构化推理和作者参与循环，提高事实准确性和针对性反驳效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在直接用于科学论文审稿反驳生成时，往往难以进行针对性反驳并保持准确的事实基础，需要结构化推理和作者干预来解决这一问题

Method: 提出DEFEND工具，明确执行自动反驳生成的底层推理过程，同时保持作者参与循环；作者只需以最小干预驱动推理过程，而非从头撰写反驳

Result: 实验和用户研究表明，直接使用LLM在事实正确性和针对性反驳方面表现较差；分段生成和带作者参与的自动化顺序方法显著提高了事实正确性和反驳强度

Conclusion: DEFEND通过结构化推理和作者参与循环，为科学论文审稿反驳生成提供了高效且认知负荷低的方法，显著优于直接使用LLM的方法

Abstract: Rebuttal generation is a critical component of the peer review process for scientific papers, enabling authors to clarify misunderstandings, correct factual inaccuracies, and guide reviewers toward a more accurate evaluation. We observe that Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle to perform targeted refutation and maintain accurate factual grounding when used directly for rebuttal generation, highlighting the need for structured reasoning and author intervention. To address this, in the paper, we introduce DEFEND an LLM based tool designed to explicitly execute the underlying reasoning process of automated rebuttal generation, while keeping the author-in-the-loop. As opposed to writing the rebuttals from scratch, the author needs to only drive the reasoning process with minimal intervention, leading an efficient approach with minimal effort and less cognitive load. We compare DEFEND against three other paradigms: (i) Direct rebuttal generation using LLM (DRG), (ii) Segment-wise rebuttal generation using LLM (SWRG), and (iii) Sequential approach (SA) of segment-wise rebuttal generation without author intervention. To enable finegrained evaluation, we extend the ReviewCritique dataset, creating review segmentation, deficiency, error type annotations, rebuttal-action labels, and mapping to gold rebuttal segments. Experimental results and a user study demonstrate that directly using LLMs perform poorly in factual correctness and targeted refutation. Segment-wise generation and the automated sequential approach with author-in-the-loop, substantially improve factual correctness and strength of refutation.

</details>


### [138] [Heterogeneous Debate Engine: Identity-Grounded Cognitive Architecture for Resilient LLM-Based Ethical Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27404)
*Jakub Masłowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HDE架构通过ID-RAG确保教义保真度和启发式心智理论进行对手建模，解决了多智能体伦理辅导中的语义漂移和逻辑退化问题，显著提升了辩论复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的多智能体系统在复杂推理任务中存在语义漂移、逻辑退化问题，特别是在需要精确答案的伦理辅导场景中，容易陷入辩证停滞、递归一致或循环论证，需要平衡教义保真度与辩证推理所需的生成灵活性。

Method: 提出异构辩论引擎(HDE)，结合身份基础的检索增强生成(ID-RAG)确保教义保真度，以及启发式心智理论进行战略对手建模，通过架构异质性维持辩论稳定性。

Result: 评估显示架构异质性是稳定性的关键变量：对立教义初始化（如义务论vs功利主义）使学生的论证复杂度得分相比基线提高了一个数量级，验证了ID-RAG和启发式心智理论在高保真对抗式教学中的有效性。

Conclusion: HDE架构通过ID-RAG和启发式心智理论的结合，成功解决了多智能体伦理辅导中的教义保真度与生成灵活性平衡问题，为对抗式教学提供了有效的认知架构解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being increasingly used as autonomous agents in complex reasoning tasks, opening the niche for dialectical interactions. However, Multi-Agent systems implemented with systematically unconstrained systems systematically undergo semantic drift and logical deterioration and thus can hardly be used in providing ethical tutoring where a precise answer is required. Current simulation often tends to degenerate into dialectical stagnation, the agents degenerate into recursive concurrence or circular arguments. A critical challenge remains: how to enforce doctrinal fidelity without suppressing the generative flexibility required for dialectical reasoning? To address this niche, we contribute the Heterogeneous Debate Engine (HDE), a cognitive architecture that combines Identity-Grounded Retrieval-Augmented Generation (ID-RAG) for doctrinal fidelity and Heuristic Theory of Mind for strategic opponent modeling. Our evaluation shows that architectural heterogeneity is a crucial variable to stability: contrary doctrinal initializations (e.g., Deontology vs. Utilitarianism) have increased the Argument Complexity Scores of students by an order of magnitude, over baselines. These findings validate the effectiveness of ID-RAG and Heuristic ToM as architectural requirements in maintaining high-fidelity (adversarial) pedagogy.

</details>


### [139] [AstraAI: LLMs, Retrieval, and AST-Guided Assistance for HPC Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27423)
*Mahesh Natarajan,Xiaoye Li,Weiqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AstraAI是一个用于高性能计算软件开发的命令行编码框架，通过集成LLM、RAG和AST分析实现上下文感知的代码生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复杂科学代码库中代码生成的挑战，提供能够理解现有项目结构和编程模式的高保真代码生成工具

Method: 构建高保真提示，通过RAG从底层框架代码库检索相关代码片段，结合AST分析提取结构上下文，集成LLM进行推理

Result: 在AMReX（DOE支持的高性能计算软件基础设施）的代表性HPC代码生成任务中进行了演示

Conclusion: AstraAI能够生成与现有项目结构和编程模式对齐的代码，支持本地和API模型，适用于HPC环境

Abstract: We present AstraAI, a command-line interface (CLI) coding framework for high-performance computing (HPC) software development. AstraAI operates directly within a Linux terminal and integrates large language models (LLMs) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Abstract Syntax Tree (AST)-based structural analysis to enable context-aware code generation for complex scientific codebases. The central idea is to construct a high-fidelity prompt that is passed to the LLM for inference. This prompt augments the user request with relevant code snippets retrieved from the underlying framework codebase via RAG and structural context extracted from AST analysis, providing the model with precise information about relevant functions, data structures, and overall code organization. The framework is designed to perform scoped modifications to source code while preserving structural consistency with the surrounding code. AstraAI supports both locally hosted models from Hugging Face and API-based frontier models accessible via the American Science Cloud, enabling flexible deployment across HPC environments. The system generates code that aligns with existing project structures and programming patterns. We demonstrate AstraAI on representative HPC code generation tasks within AMReX, a DOE-supported HPC software infrastructure for exascale applications.

</details>


### [140] [The Novelty Bottleneck: A Framework for Understanding Human Effort Scaling in AI-Assisted Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27438)
*Jacky Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个"新颖性瓶颈"模型，解释人机协作中人类努力如何随任务规模扩展，发现人类努力呈现O(E)到O(1)的急剧转变，没有中间缩放类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人机协作中人类努力的缩放规律，解释为什么在某些任务中AI无法完全消除人类参与，并澄清关于智能爆炸和"数据中心天才国度"的流行叙事。

Method: 提出一个形式化模型，将任务分解为原子决策，其中ν比例是"新颖的"(不在AI先验知识范围内)。模型假设规范制定、验证和错误修正都随任务规模缩放，从这些假设推导出缩放规律。

Result: 推导出五个非直观结果：1)人类努力没有平滑的亚线性区域；2)更好的AI只改善系数而非指数；3)团队最优规模随AI能力下降；4)挂钟时间可达O(√E)但总人类努力仍为O(E)；5)AI安全配置不对称：在前沿研究中受瓶颈限制，在利用现有知识时不受限。

Conclusion: 新颖性比例ν是控制AI辅助生产力的关键参数，该框架澄清而非反驳了关于智能爆炸的流行叙事，为理解人机协作的缩放限制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We propose a stylized model of human-AI collaboration that isolates a mechanism we call the novelty bottleneck: the fraction of a task requiring human judgment creates an irreducible serial component analogous to Amdahl's Law in parallel computing. The model assumes that tasks decompose into atomic decisions, a fraction $ν$ of which are "novel" (not covered by the agent's prior), and that specification, verification, and error correction each scale with task size. From these assumptions, we derive several non-obvious consequences: (1) there is no smooth sublinear regime for human effort it transitions sharply from $O(E)$ to $O(1)$ with no intermediate scaling class; (2) better agents improve the coefficient on human effort but not the exponent; (3) for organizations of n humans with AI agents, optimal team size decreases with agent capability; (4) wall-clock time achieves $O(\sqrt{E})$ through team parallelism but total human effort remains $O(E)$; and (5) the resulting AI safety profile is asymmetric -- AI is bottlenecked on frontier research but unbottlenecked on exploiting existing knowledge. We show these predictions are consistent with empirical observations from AI coding benchmarks, scientific productivity data, and practitioner reports. Our contribution is not a proof that human effort must scale linearly, but a framework that identifies the novelty fraction as the key parameter governing AI-assisted productivity, and derives consequences that clarify -- rather than refute -- prevalent narratives about intelligence explosions and the "country of geniuses in a data center."

</details>


### [141] [Dual-Stage LLM Framework for Scenario-Centric Semantic Interpretation in Driving Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27536)
*Jean Douglas Carvalho,Hugo Taciro Kenji,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Glaucia Melo,Max Mauro Dias Santos,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个基于场景的框架，用于审计LLM在城市场景中的风险推理能力，通过构建确定性、时间有界的场景窗口，在固定提示约束下比较不同模型的风险评估表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ADAS系统依赖基于学习的感知，但安全相关故障往往源于部分可观测性和语义模糊性，而非组件故障。需要可复现的审计方法来评估LLM在风险推理中的表现，特别是在语义模糊场景下的决策一致性。

Method: 构建场景中心框架，从多模态驾驶数据创建确定性、时间有界的场景窗口。在固定提示约束和封闭数值风险模式（closed numeric risk schema）下评估模型，确保结构化、可比较的输出。在精选的"近人"场景集上比较两个纯文本模型和一个多模态模型。

Result: 结果显示模型间在严重程度分配、高风险升级、证据使用和因果归因方面存在系统性差异。分歧延伸到对弱势道路使用者的解释，表明变异性通常反映内在语义不确定性而非孤立模型故障。

Conclusion: 研究强调了场景中心审计的重要性，以及在将LLM推理集成到安全对齐的驾驶辅助系统时，需要明确的模糊性管理策略。

Abstract: Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS) increasingly rely on learning-based perception, yet safety-relevant failures often arise without component malfunction, driven instead by partial observability and semantic ambiguity in how risk is interpreted and communicated. This paper presents a scenario-centric framework for reproducible auditing of LLM-based risk reasoning in urban driving contexts. Deterministic, temporally bounded scenario windows are constructed from multimodal driving data and evaluated under fixed prompt constraints and a closed numeric risk schema, ensuring structured and comparable outputs across models. Experiments on a curated near-people scenario set compare two text-only models and one multimodal model under identical inputs and prompts. Results reveal systematic inter-model divergence in severity assignment, high-risk escalation, evidence use, and causal attribution. Disagreement extends to the interpretation of vulnerable road user presence, indicating that variability often reflects intrinsic semantic indeterminacy rather than isolated model failure. These findings highlight the importance of scenario-centric auditing and explicit ambiguity management when integrating LLM-based reasoning into safety-aligned driver assistance systems.

</details>


### [142] [From indicators to biology: the calibration problem in artificial consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27597)
*Florentin Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文认为当前基于意识理论的指标来评估AI意识的方法存在缺陷，主张转向基于生物系统的工程方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能意识评估从行为测试转向内部架构分析，基于意识理论推导指标并更新置信度，这比天真的图灵式测试有所进步。但指标方法存在认识论校准不足的问题：意识科学理论碎片化、指标缺乏独立验证、人工现象性缺乏真实基准。在这些条件下，对当前AI系统进行概率性意识归因为时过早。

Method: 提出重新定向研究策略，转向基于生物系统的工程方法，包括生物混合系统、神经形态系统和连接组规模系统，这些方法能够缩小与唯一经验锚定意识领域（生命系统）的差距。

Result: 分析表明当前基于指标的AI意识评估方法存在根本性缺陷，需要更可靠的替代方案。

Conclusion: 在现有条件下，对当前AI系统进行概率性意识归因为时过早。更可辩护的近期策略是转向基于生物系统的工程方法，通过生物混合、神经形态和连接组规模系统来缩小与生命系统意识经验基础的差距。

Abstract: Recent work on artificial consciousness shifts evaluation from behaviour to internal architecture, deriving indicators from theories of consciousness and updating credences accordingly. This is progress beyond naive Turing-style tests. But the indicator-based programme remains epistemically under-calibrated: consciousness science is theoretically fragmented, indicators lack independent validation, and no ground truth of artificial phenomenality exists. Under these conditions, probabilistic consciousness attribution to current AI systems is premature. A more defensible near-term strategy is to redirect effort toward biologically grounded engineering -- biohybrid, neuromorphic, and connectome-scale systems -- that reduces the gap with the only domain where consciousness is empirically anchored: living systems.

</details>


### [143] [What does a system modify when it modifies itself?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27611)
*Florentin Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个区分自我修改系统类型的框架，包含规则层次、固定核心和三种规则类型，识别了四种修改机制，揭示了人类与AI在自我表示和因果访问上的交叉不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学和人工智能领域都研究自我修改系统，但缺乏统一的框架来区分不同类型的自我修改（如执行控制、元认知、层次学习）。需要形式化框架来比较生物认知与人工系统的自我修改机制。

Method: 构建了一个最小结构框架：包含规则层次、固定核心，区分有效规则、表示规则和因果可访问规则。识别了四种修改机制：无修改行动、低层修改、结构修改和目的论修订。将框架应用于人类认知和人工系统进行比较分析。

Result: 发现人类与AI在自我表示和因果访问上存在交叉不对称性：人类自我表示和因果能力集中在高层级，操作层级不透明；而AI在操作层级有丰富表示和因果访问，但最高评估层级缺乏这些能力。这为人类-AI比较提供了结构特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为比较生物与人工自我修改系统提供了统一基础，揭示了结构不对称性，对人工意识研究有启示（高阶理论和注意图式理论是特例），提出了四个可检验预测和四个开放问题。

Abstract: When a cognitive system modifies its own functioning, what exactly does it modify: a low-level rule, a control rule, or the norm that evaluates its own revisions? Cognitive science describes executive control, metacognition, and hierarchical learning with precision, but lacks a formal framework distinguishing these targets of transformation. Contemporary artificial intelligence likewise exhibits self-modification without common criteria for comparison with biological cognition.
  We show that the question of what counts as a self-modifying system entails a minimal structure: a hierarchy of rules, a fixed core, and a distinction between effective rules, represented rules, and causally accessible rules. Four regimes are identified: (1) action without modification, (2) low-level modification, (3) structural modification, and (4) teleological revision. Each regime is anchored in a cognitive phenomenon and a corresponding artificial system.
  Applied to humans, the framework yields a central result: a crossing of opacities. Humans have self-representation and causal power concentrated at upper hierarchical levels, while operational levels remain largely opaque. Reflexive artificial systems display the inverse profile: rich representation and causal access at operational levels, but none at the highest evaluative level. This crossed asymmetry provides a structural signature for human-AI comparison. The framework also offers insight into artificial consciousness, with higher-order theories and Attention Schema Theory as special cases. We derive four testable predictions and identify four open problems: the independence of transformativity and autonomy, the viability of self-modification, the teleological lock, and identity under transformation.

</details>


### [144] [DSevolve: Enabling Real-Time Adaptive Scheduling on Dynamic Shop Floor with LLM-Evolved Heuristic Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27628)
*Jin Huang,Jie Yang,XinLei Zhou,Qihao Liu,Liang Gao,Xinyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSevolve：一种工业调度框架，通过离线演化质量多样的调度规则组合，并在线上自适应部署，以应对动态制造环境中的扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态制造环境中的机器故障和新订单到达等扰动会持续改变最优调度策略，需要自适应规则选择。现有的基于LLM的自动启发式设计框架演化单一精英规则，无法满足这种适应性需求。

Method: DSevolve采用离线演化质量多样的调度规则组合，通过多角色种子和拓扑感知进化算子生成行为多样的规则档案，使用MAP-Elites特征空间索引。在线部署时，基于探针的指纹机制表征当前车间状态，从离线知识库检索高质量候选规则，并通过快速前瞻模拟选择最佳规则。

Result: 在基于真实工业数据生成的500个动态柔性作业车间实例上评估，DSevolve优于最先进的AHD框架、经典调度规则、遗传编程和深度强化学习方法。

Conclusion: DSevolve为智能车间调度提供了一个实用且可部署的解决方案，通过质量多样性演化组合和自适应在线部署，实现了秒级响应时间。

Abstract: In dynamic manufacturing environments, disruptions such as machine breakdowns and new order arrivals continuously shift the optimal dispatching strategy, making adaptive rule selection essential. Existing LLM-powered Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD) frameworks evolve toward a single elite rule that cannot meet this adaptability demand. To address this, we present DSevolve, an industrial scheduling framework that evolves a quality-diverse portfolio of dispatching rules offline and adaptively deploys them online with second-level response time. Multi-persona seeding and topology-aware evolutionary operators produce a behaviorally diverse rule archive indexed by a MAP-Elites feature space. Upon each disruption event, a probe-based fingerprinting mechanism characterizes the current shop floor state, retrieves high-quality candidate rules from an offline knowledge base, and selects the best one via rapid look-ahead simulation. Evaluated on 500 dynamic flexible job shop instances derived from real industrial data, DSevolve outperforms state-of-the-art AHD frameworks, classical dispatching rules, genetic programming, and deep reinforcement learning, offering a practical and deployable solution for intelligent shop floor scheduling.

</details>


### [145] [TianJi:An autonomous AI meteorologist for discovering physical mechanisms in atmospheric science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27738)
*Kaikai Zhang,Xiang Wang,Haoluo Zhao,Nan Chen,Mengyang Yu Jing-Jia Luo,Tao Song,Fan Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TianJi是首个能够自主驱动复杂数值模型验证物理机制的"AI气象学家"系统，通过大语言模型驱动的多智能体架构，将科研解耦为认知规划和工程执行，将研究周期压缩至数小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在天气预报中虽取得突破，但本质上仍是统计拟合，难以揭示大气物理因果机制。面向物理机制的研究仍严重依赖领域知识和人工繁琐操作，成为限制地球系统科学探索效率的瓶颈。

Method: 提出TianJi系统，采用大语言模型驱动的多智能体架构：1）自主进行文献研究和生成科学假设；2）将科研解耦为认知规划（元规划器解释假设并设计实验路线图）和工程执行（专业工作者智能体协作完成数据准备、模型配置和多维结果分析）。

Result: 在两个经典大气动力学场景（飑线冷池和台风路径偏转）中，TianJi实现了专家级端到端实验操作，零人工干预，将研究周期压缩至数小时。系统提供详细结果分析，并自主判断和解释假设的有效性。

Conclusion: TianJi揭示了AI在地球系统科学中的角色正从"黑盒预测器"转变为"可解释的科学协作者"，为科学机制的高通量探索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has achieved breakthroughs comparable to traditional numerical models in data-driven weather forecasting, yet it remains essentially statistical fitting and struggles to uncover the physical causal mechanisms of the atmosphere. Physics-oriented mechanism research still heavily relies on domain knowledge and cumbersome engineering operations of human scientists, becoming a bottleneck restricting the efficiency of Earth system science exploration. Here, we propose TianJi - the first "AI meteorologist" system capable of autonomously driving complex numerical models to verify physical mechanisms. Powered by a large language model-driven multi-agent architecture, TianJi can autonomously conduct literature research and generate scientific hypotheses. We further decouple scientific research into cognitive planning and engineering execution: the meta-planner interprets hypotheses and devises experimental roadmaps, while a cohort of specialized worker agents collaboratively complete data preparation, model configuration, and multi-dimensional result analysis. In two classic atmospheric dynamic scenarios (squall-line cold pools and typhoon track deflections), TianJi accomplishes expert-level end-to-end experimental operations with zero human intervention, compressing the research cycle to a few hours. It also delivers detailed result analyses and autonomously judges and explains the validity of the hypotheses from outputs. TianJi reveals that the role of AI in Earth system science is transitioning from a "black-box predictor" to an "interpretable scientific collaborator", offering a new paradigm for high-throughput exploration of scientific mechanisms.

</details>


### [146] [SkyNet: Belief-Aware Planning for Partially-Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27751)
*Adam Haile*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkyNet（Belief-Aware MuZero）通过添加自我条件化的辅助头（赢家预测和排名估计）来增强MuZero在部分可观测环境中的表现，无需显式信念状态跟踪或搜索算法修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MuZero在完美信息游戏中表现出色，但在部分可观测、随机、多玩家环境中表现有限。这类环境在纸牌游戏、自主谈判、金融交易和多智能体机器人等领域普遍存在。MuZero的潜在编码缺乏专门机制来表示未观测变量的不确定性。

Method: 在标准MuZero架构基础上添加自我条件化的辅助头，包括赢家预测和排名估计。这些目标鼓励潜在状态保留在部分可观测性下可预测结果的信息，无需显式信念状态跟踪或搜索算法修改。使用决策粒度环境、基于transformer的编码以及启发式对手的自对弈课程。

Result: 在Skyjo（部分可观测、非零和、随机纸牌游戏）的1000局头对头评估中，SkyNet达到75.3%的峰值胜率（相对于基线+194 Elo，p < 10^{-50}）。对启发式对手的胜率也优于基线（0.720 vs. 0.466）。信念感知模型最初表现不如基线，但在训练吞吐量足够后显著超越。

Conclusion: 信念感知的辅助监督在部分可观测性下改进了学习表示，但需要足够的数据流。该方法无需显式信念建模或搜索算法修改，通过辅助目标增强潜在状态表示，有效处理部分可观测环境中的不确定性。

Abstract: In 2019, Google DeepMind released MuZero, a model-based reinforcement learning method that achieves strong results in perfect-information games by combining learned dynamics models with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). However, comparatively little work has extended MuZero to partially observable, stochastic, multi-player environments, where agents must act under uncertainty about hidden state. Such settings arise not only in card games but in domains such as autonomous negotiation, financial trading, and multi-agent robotics. In the absence of explicit belief modeling, MuZero's latent encoding has no dedicated mechanism for representing uncertainty over unobserved variables.
  To address this, we introduce SkyNet (Belief-Aware MuZero), which adds ego-conditioned auxiliary heads for winner prediction and rank estimation to the standard MuZero architecture. These objectives encourage the latent state to retain information predictive of outcomes under partial observability, without requiring explicit belief-state tracking or changes to the search algorithm.
  We evaluate SkyNet on Skyjo, a partially observable, non-zero-sum, stochastic card game, using a decision-granularity environment, transformer-based encoding, and a curriculum of heuristic opponents with self-play. In 1000-game head-to-head evaluations at matched checkpoints, SkyNet achieves a 75.3% peak win rate against the baseline (+194 Elo, $p < 10^{-50}$). SkyNet also outperforms the baseline against heuristic opponents (0.720 vs.\ 0.466 win rate). Critically, the belief-aware model initially underperforms the baseline but decisively surpasses it once training throughput is sufficient, suggesting that belief-aware auxiliary supervision improves learned representations under partial observability, but only given adequate data flow.

</details>


### [147] [GEAKG: Generative Executable Algorithm Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27922)
*Camilo Chacón Sartori,José H. García,Andrei Voicu Tomut,Christian Blum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GEAKG：一种将算法设计知识表示为可执行、可学习知识图谱的新框架，支持跨领域零样本迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法设计中的过程性知识（算法设计和算子组合的know-how）通常隐含在代码中，难以重用和跨领域迁移。现有知识图谱范式对可执行、可学习的过程性知识表示支持有限。

Method: 提出生成式可执行算法知识图谱（GEAKG），其节点存储可执行算子，边编码学习到的组合模式，图谱遍历可生成解决方案。采用三层架构：1）由LLM生成拓扑和算子的生成层；2）基于蚁群优化的学习引擎；3）可插拔本体（RoleSchema）参数化。框架在引擎层面与领域无关。

Result: 通过两个无共享领域特定代码的案例研究验证：1）在70个跨数据集迁移对上的神经架构搜索；2）组合优化中，从旅行商问题零样本迁移到调度和分配领域。结果支持算法专业知识可作为可执行知识图谱进行显式表示、学习和迁移。

Conclusion: GEAKG框架证明算法专业知识能够被显式表示为可执行、可学习、可迁移的知识图谱结构，为过程性知识的系统化组织和重用提供了新范式。

Abstract: In the context of algorithms for problem solving, procedural knowledge -- the know-how of algorithm design and operator composition -- remains implicit in code, lost between runs, and must be re-engineered for each new domain. Knowledge graphs (KGs) have proven effective for organizing declarative knowledge, yet current KG paradigms provide limited support for representing procedural knowledge as executable, learnable graph structures. We introduce \textit{Generative Executable Algorithm Knowledge Graphs} (GEAKG), a class of KGs whose nodes store executable operators, whose edges encode learned composition patterns, and whose traversal generates solutions. A GEAKG is \emph{generative} (topology and operators are synthesized by a Large Language Model), \emph{executable} (every node is runnable code), and \emph{transferable} (learned patterns generalize zero-shot across domains). The framework is domain-agnostic at the engine level: the same three-layer architecture and Ant Colony Optimization (ACO)-based learning engine can be instantiated across domains, parameterized by a pluggable ontology (\texttt{RoleSchema}). Two case studies -- sharing no domain-specific framework code -- provide concrete evidence for this framework hypothesis: (1)~Neural Architecture Search across 70 cross-dataset transfer pairs on two tabular benchmarks, and (2)~Combinatorial Optimization, where knowledge learned on the Traveling Salesman Problem transfers zero-shot to scheduling and assignment domains. Taken together, the results support that algorithmic expertise can be explicitly represented, learned, and transferred as executable knowledge graphs.

</details>


### [148] [CARV: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Compositional Analogical Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27958)
*Yongkang Du,Xiaohan Zou,Minhao Cheng,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARV是首个评估多模态大语言模型组合类比推理能力的诊断基准，包含5,500个样本，揭示当前模型与人类水平存在显著差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多模态大语言模型类比推理能力的评估忽略了从多个来源组合规则的能力，这是高阶智能的关键组成部分

Method: 提出CARV任务和数据集，将类比从单对扩展到多对，要求模型从每对中提取符号规则并组合新变换

Result: 最先进的MLLMs表现显著不足：Gemini-2.5 Pro仅40.4%准确率，远低于人类100%水平；诊断分析显示两个一致失败模式

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在组合类比推理任务上存在严重局限，特别是在将视觉变化分解为符号规则和在多样化复杂设置下保持鲁棒性方面

Abstract: Analogical reasoning tests a fundamental aspect of human cognition: mapping the relation from one pair of objects to another. Existing evaluations of this ability in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) overlook the ability to compose rules from multiple sources, a critical component of higher-order intelligence. To close this gap, we introduce CARV (Compositional Analogical Reasoning in Vision), a novel task together with a 5,500-sample dataset as the first diagnostic benchmark. We extend the analogy from a single pair to multiple pairs, which requires MLLMs to extract symbolic rules from each pair and compose new transformations. Evaluation on the state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals a striking performance gap: even Gemini-2.5 Pro achieving only 40.4% accuracy, far below human-level performance of 100%. Diagnostic analysis shows two consistent failure modes: (1) decomposing visual changes into symbolic rules, and (2) maintaining robustness under diverse or complex settings, highlighting the limitations of current MLLMs on this task.

</details>


### [149] [HeteroHub: An Applicable Data Management Framework for Heterogeneous Multi-Embodied Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28010)
*Xujia Li,Xin Li,Junquan Huang,Beirong Cui,Zibin Wu,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HeteroHub框架，为异构多具身智能体系统提供统一的数据管理基础设施，支持静态知识、多模态训练数据和实时传感器流数据的集成与管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多具身智能体系统需要协调具有不同能力的多个具身智能体在动态环境中完成任务，这涉及大量异构数据的收集、生成和消费。现有框架缺乏统一的数据管理基础设施来支持这类系统的实际部署。

Method: 提出HeteroHub数据中心框架，集成静态元数据、任务对齐的训练语料库和实时数据流。该框架支持任务感知的模型训练、上下文敏感的执行以及由真实世界反馈驱动的闭环控制。

Result: 在演示中，HeteroHub成功协调多个具身AI智能体执行复杂任务，展示了强大的数据管理框架如何实现可扩展、可维护和可演化的具身AI系统。

Conclusion: HeteroHub框架为异构多具身智能体系统提供了统一的数据管理解决方案，解决了现有框架在数据管理基础设施方面的不足，能够支持这类系统的实际部署和规模化应用。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Multi-Embodied Agent Systems involve coordinating multiple embodied agents with diverse capabilities to accomplish tasks in dynamic environments. This process requires the collection, generation, and consumption of massive, heterogeneous data, which primarily falls into three categories: static knowledge regarding the agents, tasks, and environments; multimodal training datasets tailored for various AI models; and high-frequency sensor streams. However, existing frameworks lack a unified data management infrastructure to support the real-world deployment of such systems. To address this gap, we present \textbf{HeteroHub}, a data-centric framework that integrates static metadata, task-aligned training corpora, and real-time data streams. The framework supports task-aware model training, context-sensitive execution, and closed-loop control driven by real-world feedback. In our demonstration, HeteroHub successfully coordinates multiple embodied AI agents to execute complex tasks, illustrating how a robust data management framework can enable scalable, maintainable, and evolvable embodied AI systems.

</details>


### [150] [When Choices Become Priors: Contrastive Decoding for Scientific Figure Multiple-Choice QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28026)
*Taeyun Roh,Eun-yeong Jo,Wonjune Jang,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCICON是一种无需训练的解码方法，通过从图像条件得分中减去纯文本选项得分来减少科学多选题中选项本身带来的先验偏差，从而提高基于图像证据的推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像多选题（MCQA）存在一个特殊偏差：答案选项本身可以作为先验，引导多模态模型倾向于科学上看似合理的选项，即使图像证据支持不同的答案。作者希望解决这种基于选项先验而非图像证据的推理偏差。

Method: 提出SCICON方法，这是一种无需训练的解码策略。对每个候选答案，计算其图像条件得分减去纯文本选项得分。通过这种对比解码方式，直接针对候选文本中编码的选择诱导先验进行校正，使模型更依赖图像证据而非文本先验。

Result: 在三个科学图像问答基准测试和三个模型骨干上，SCICON相比标准解码基线持续提高了准确性。这表明通过解码对抗选择诱导先验是改进科学MCQA中基于图像推理的有效且简单的方法。

Conclusion: SCICON通过直接针对候选文本中的选择诱导先验进行解码校正，有效减少了科学图像多选题中的偏差，提高了基于图像证据的推理能力，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Scientific figure multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) requires models to reason over diverse visual evidence, ranging from charts and multipanel figures to microscopy and biomedical images. However, this setting suffers from a distinctive bias: answer choices themselves can act as priors, steering multimodal models toward scientifically plausible options even when the figure supports a different answer. We investigate this failure mode through a simple question: what if decoding explicitly discounts what the model would prefer from text alone, so as to favor figure-grounded evidence? To this end, we propose SCICON, a training-free decoding method that scores each candidate by subtracting a text-only option score from its image-conditioned counterpart. Unlike prior contrastive decoding approaches that mitigate hallucinations by contrasting original inputs with distorted images or perturbed instructions, SCICON directly targets the choice-induced prior encoded in candidate text. Across three scientific figure QA benchmarks and three model backbones, SCICON consistently improves accuracy over standard decoding baselines.
